# The specforge-only worklist (programme "SpecForge serves itself", .planning/serves-itself-2026-11-01).
# One row per command a shell fence, an inline run or a rendered prompt (`prompt:<id>`) runs that is not specforge
# or the shell's own: <file>\t<tool>\t<line>. Tests: port_tools::a_shell_block_calls_only_specforge and
# rendered_lines::a_rendered_prompt_calls_only_specforge. A row with no hit fails, and so does a hit with no row:
# take a row off when its line stops running the tool. Grouped by the building block that answers it.

# A shape: answers shaped for the shell: --pick [0], --count, --match, --format lines (R1). 975 rows.
